# The Book of the Orphic Hymns, together with the principal fragments of other hymns also attributed to Orpheus, the whole extracted from Hermann's edition of the Orphica

- **Title:** The Book of the Orphic Hymns, together with the principal fragments of other hymns also attributed to Orpheus, the whole extracted from Hermann's edition of the Orphica
- **Author:** Orpheus (attributed); edited by Julian Hibbert
- **Edition:** London: privately printed (Hibbert), 1827; Internet Archive item bookorphichymns00hibbgoog. The Greek text only (in capital letters, no English translation), the hymns and principal fragments extracted from Hermann's edition, with Hibbert's notes.
- **Translator:** none
- **Year:** 1827
- **Read from:** https://archive.org/download/bookorphichymns00hibbgoog/bookorphichymns00hibbgoog_djvu.txt
- **Licence / copyright status:** Public domain (published 1827).
